## 2026-06-17 — Native land purchase: pay-or-steal a native tile (Peter Minuit free) [ultracode: understand+design workflow → implement → adversarial review]

**Requested:** "Continue on" → Chris chose **land purchase** (`86d3c9tha`) as the first consumer of the native tile-ownership foundation.
**Did:** Shipped **native land purchase** (`86d3c9tha`, `8dfddb2`). Ran a **multi-agent understand+design workflow** (2 readers + design synthesis) to scope it, then implemented: **`Game.LandPrice(tile)`** = `60 (classic-medium landPriceFactor) × Σ(tile POTENTIAL yield over non-food goods) + 100`, then the `landPaymentModifier` (Peter Minuit −100% → **free**). **`ClaimLandByPaying`** (deducts the gold — nothing credited, no native treasury) / **`ClaimLandByStealing`** (+200 alarm, FreeCol `TENSION_ADD_LAND_TAKEN`, to every settlement of the robbed nation); a native-settlement tile is **not for sale**. A claimed tile is a **saved override** (`GameMap.ClaimedFromNatives`) that `ClaimNativeLand` subtracts after every re-derivation, so it never reverts; **save v26** (`ClaimedTiles`, additive/omitted-when-empty → byte-identical to v25 with no purchases). The forced found/work **trigger is deferred** (needs a pay-vs-steal UI dialog), so founding is unchanged and the soak stays byte-stable; no RNG drawn. Ran a **3-dimension adversarial review** → **2 real findings, both fixed before commit**: (1) the price was using **father-boosted `TileYield`** (Henry Hudson's +furs would inflate land cost) → added **`TileYieldPotential`** (unmodified terrain+resource yield, FreeCol `getPotentialProduction`-with-null-owner) and the price now sums that; `TileYield` = `ApplyGoodsModifiers(TileYieldPotential)` (behaviour-preserving); (2) the payment modifier used `FirstOrDefault` → now `ApplyGoodsModifiers` (stacks by index).
**Status:** **710 L1+L2 + 4 soak green** (+11 `NativeLandPurchaseTests`, incl. a Hudson-doesn't-inflate regression; save-version pins 25→26 across 7 files). Derived-ownership + the saved override round-trip byte-identically; founding unchanged → soak byte-stable. Full solution builds. Work committed `8dfddb2`; PostWorkSummary follows. ClickUp `86d3c9tha` → In Review.
**Changed:** GameLogic `GameSession/Game.cs` (`LandPrice`/`TileYieldPotential`/`CheckClaimLand`/`ClaimLandByPaying`/`ClaimLandByStealing`; `ClaimNativeLand` honours the override; `CheckFoundColony` note), `World/GameMap.cs` (`ClaimedFromNatives` + `ClaimFromNatives` + ctor param), `Persistence/SaveGame.cs` (v26 `ClaimedTiles`); tests `GameSession/NativeLandPurchaseTests.cs` (new, +11) + 7 version-pin bumps. Docs `natives.md` (both layers + changelog + verification), `save-load.md` (v26). Commit `8dfddb2`.
**Decisions:** **(1) Price from unmodified potential** (`TileYieldPotential`), not father-boosted yield — the review caught this; faithful to FreeCol. **(2) Trigger deferred** — being forced to buy/steal before founding/working on native land needs a UI choice; only the claim API ships now (founding unchanged, soak byte-stable). **(3) landPriceFactor=60** (classic-medium, the project baseline). **(4) Saved override** (`ClaimedFromNatives`, v26) layered on the derived ownership — cleanest split; bought tiles survive re-derivation. **(5) Deviations (documented):** no native treasury (gold just leaves the buyer); steal alarm nation-wide (ownership is per-nation); `ExtraClaimableRadius` not modelled.

## 2026-06-17 — Native tile ownership model (foundation) [ultracode: understand+design workflow → implement → adversarial review]

**Requested:** "Continue on" → Chris chose **native tile ownership** at the LCR-arc fork (the next planned slice, strange mounds, was blocked on it; it also unblocks native land purchase).
**Did:** Shipped the **native-land-ownership foundation** (new task `86d3cm4d6`, `6e236bc`). Ran a **multi-agent understand+design workflow** (3 readers — FreeCol claim model / our model / consumers — + a design synthesis) to scope it, then implemented: each native settlement owns the land within its `SettlementType.ClaimableRadius` (FreeCol `Tile.owner`). `World/NativeLandClaimGenerator.Claim()` walks each settlement's Chebyshev claim disc (camps/villages 1, Inca/Aztec cities 2), keeps land + in-bounds tiles, resolves overlaps to the **nearest** settlement (tie-break lowest id). `GameMap` gains a sparse `_nativeOwners` dict + `IsNativeOwned`/`NativeOwnerOf`/`NativeOwners` (+ internal `SetNativeOwner`/`ClearNativeOwner`/`ClearNativeOwners`). `Game.ClaimNativeLand()` is **idempotent** and runs at `Game.New`, `Game.Restore`, **and on native-settlement destruction** in `AttackSettlement`. Ownership is a pure, RNG-free function of positions + radii → **DERIVED, never saved** (no save bump, no RNG stream touched). Modeled faithfully: `UnitType.BuildCost`/`RequiredPopulation`/`RequiredAbilities`/`BuildLimit` + `BuildingType` scoped build abilities (`BuildableUnitTypeIds`/`BuildsNavalUnits`, down the `extends` chain); a unified `Buildable` gate (`ResolveBuildable`/`BuildRefusal`) where units add the build-ability scope + cap gates and **omit** the building-only ones; `SpawnUnit` owner overload. **Ships + the free colonist excluded** (deferred / non-buildable).

## 2026-06-16 — Colony capture plunder [queue slice 5 · sack the treasury]

**Requested:** Autonomous overnight — keep shipping. Took the deferred **plunder gold on capture** now that I'd pinned the FreeCol `getPlunderRange` formula (found during the pillage research) — a contained, faithful slice building on the capture work.
**Did:** A winning colony assault now **sacks the former owner's treasury**. `Game.PlunderColony` runs in the `AttackColony` win branch before the handover; `ColonyPlunderAmount` = FreeCol `getPlunderRange` (`upper = ownerGold×(colonyPop+1)/(coloniesPop+1)`, payout `rnd[0,upper]+1`, 0 if the owner is broke), capped at the victim's purse (no negative gold), transferred former-owner → captor. The draw is on the **captor's** stream — human stream 0 for a human capture, the power's own stream for a foreign-AI capture — so foreign capture never touches the human's stream 0 (ADR-009, triage-confirmed).

Correct for percentage/multiplicative scoped modifiers (the only one is Drake).

## 2026-06-15 — Privateer piracy [next-5 plan, slice 2 · 1c-3d-i]

**Requested:** "get to it, do not stop" → next-5 slice 2: **privateer piracy** (`86d3buvfa`).
**Did:** A ship with **`model.ability.piracy`** (the privateer) can now **raid a rival colonial power without declaring war** — `Game.Attack` skips the colonial War/tension hook when the attacker *or* the defender has piracy (so you can plunder rival shipping deniably, and attacking someone else's privateer also stays peaceful; an existing war is left as-is, not reset). A privateer also **flies no flag**: when a foreign privateer raids the human, the `CombatNotice` carries the sentinel `Game.UnknownEnemyNationId` and the HUD names no nation ("A privateer sank your frigate"). `UnitType.Piracy` parses the ability. This is the *mechanic* (the human can use it now); **AI-initiated** piracy from peace is deferred (foreign fleets have no privateers + the AI only attacks at war). The FreeCol `attackedByPrivateers` player flag is **not modelled** (its only consumer is a monarch naval-support offer we don't have).
**Status:** **509 tests green** (477 L1+L2 + 4 soak + 28 scene; +7: `NavalCombatTests` — privateer raids without war / attacking a privateer doesn't war / a non-pirate attack still wars / a foreign privateer raid hides its nation; `CombatModelTests` `piracy` pinned to spec). Build 0/0. Save format unchanged (v21); fully deterministic (no RNG; foreign privateer draws from its own stream → stream 0 untouched). Process: FreeCol research (`csCombat` piracy branch, `isOwnerHidden`) → implement → **2-lens adversarial review** (fidelity + determinism/regression) → **no must-fix, no should-fix**; applied the one actionable nit (a defensive comment: a privateer attacking a native would still raise native tension, but that path is unreachable today — naval-vs-land gate + no native ships). The other nits (no `attackedByPrivateers` consumer; AI-initiated piracy) are already-documented deferrals.
**Changed:** `Specification/UnitType.cs`+`Ruleset.cs` (`Piracy`); `GameSession/Game.cs` (war-hook suppression, `UnknownEnemyNationId` sentinel, `AttackHumanUnit` flag-hiding); `presentation/GameController.cs` (`FormatCombatNotice` anonymous-privateer phrasing); tests `NavalCombatTests.cs`/`CombatModelTests.cs`; docs `combat.md`/`diplomacy.md`/`game-logic.md`/`presentation.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** Collapse FreeCol's two piracy branches into one "skip the war/tension hook" guard (attacker- and defender-side). Hide the flag via a sentinel `AttackerNationId` (the presentation renders "privateer") — `model.nation.unknownEnemy` is a parsed-and-skipped pseudo-nation, so a sentinel string, not an object. No persisted state.
**Scheduled next:** **slice 3 — 1c-3d-ii Francis Drake** (`86d3buvfa`): +50% offence/defence scoped to privateers — the **first scoped father combat modifier** (`FatherModifier` needs a `Scope` field; `ParseModifier` currently drops `<scope>`; first father fold into `OffenceBase`/`DefenceBase`). Save-stable. Then slice 4 colony-tile attack path, slice 5 ambient native alarm.
**Follow-ups:** Drake (slice 3); AI-initiated piracy (foreign privateer fleets raiding at peace); the rest of the next-5; colony capture (#6).
**Needs you:** Nothing blocking. Privateers can now raid rival shipping without starting a war (and you can't tell whose privateer just hit you).

## 2026-06-15 — Native interaction UI [next-5 plan, slice 1]

**Did:** Clicking a discovered native settlement now opens a small **interaction panel** instead of immediately attacking. The panel (`NativeSettlementPanel`) offers **Speak with chief**, **Learn skill**, and **Attack** — each shown only when its `Check…` allows the selected unit (Speak hidden once visited; Learn hidden if the skill's consumed / you're not a colonist / the settlement is too hostile; a non-adjacent or absent unit gets a hint instead). Pure presentation (ADR-006): every gate is a `Check`, every effect a Game oracle (`Visit`/`LearnSkill`/`AttackSettlement`); no logic/save/RNG change. `HandleTileClick` routes a discovered-settlement click (fog-gated on `IsExplored`) to the panel and the now-unused `AttackSettlementAt` was removed.

Build 0/0. GameLogic untouched (presentation-only); save format unchanged (v21). (One test-only wrinkle: asserting the pressed button *disappears* hit the Godot "free a node mid-signal-emit lingers a frame" quirk; switched to asserting the info-label rebuilt — which proves the same re-gate — and it passed cleanly, confirming no real bug.)
**Changed:** `presentation/NativeSettlementPanel.cs` (new), `presentation/GameController.cs` (click routing + `_nativePanel` + `OpenNativeSettlementPanel`; removed `AttackSettlementAt`), `scenes/main.tscn` (panel node, load_steps 6→7), tests `presentation/tests/InputTests.cs`; docs `natives.md`/`presentation.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** **Click-to-open-panel** replaces click-to-attack at a native settlement (faithful to the original game's settlement dialog + safer — no accidental assaults; flagged to Chris, reversible). On-map native **trade** (selling a ship's cargo, `SellToNatives`) is **not** surfaced yet — a follow-up; this slice scopes to speak/learn/attack.
**Scheduled next:** **slice 2 — 1c-3d-i privateer piracy** (`86d3bpzuj`): a `model.ability.piracy` unit attacks a rival without declaring war + hidden nationality in the notice; save-stable. Then slice 3 Francis Drake, slice 4 colony-tile attack path, slice 5 ambient native alarm.

## 2026-06-15 — Naval cargo loot (captureGoods) [program #1, slice 1c-3c]

**Requested:** "Begin working and DO NOT stop" → the next naval sub-slice: cargo **loot** (`86d3bqdhq`, split from the 1c-3c/d/e follow-ups task).
**Did:** A winning **naval raider** now **plunders** the beaten ship. When a ship wins a ship-vs-ship fight, if the winner has **`model.ability.captureGoods`** (frigate, privateer, man-o-war — the pure transports don't) and **free hold space**, it takes as much of the loser's cargo as fits **before** the loser sinks (1c-3a) or limps to repair (1c-3b); the rest is lost with the ship (FreeCol `csLootShip`). The transfer (`Game.LootShip`) packs goods 100/slot, merging into any partial stack the winner already holds, and stops when the hold is full — deterministic (we auto-take what fits; FreeCol's loot-cargo *chooser* dialog is a single-player nicety with no AI equivalent). `UnitType.CaptureGoods` parses the ability. **No save-format change** — looted goods ride the existing `Cargo` serialization (still v21). The path draws **no RNG**, so a foreign raider looting the human's ship never touches stream 0 (ADR-009).
**Status:** **501 tests green** (470 L1+L2 + 4 soak + 27 scene; +10: `NavalCombatTests` — a privateer loots a cargo ship it sinks; damage also loots; loot capped by free hold space; loot capped at full capacity; a no-`captureGoods` winner doesn't loot — + `CombatModelTests` `captureGoods` pinned to spec, 5 cases). Build 0/0. Save format unchanged (v21). Process: FreeCol research (pinned `resolveAttack` adds `LOOT_SHIP` before sink/damage when `winner.captureGoods && !loser.goods.isEmpty`; `csLootShip` takes up to free space; `captureGoods` on frigate/privateer/manOWar/flyingDutchman) → implement → **2-lens adversarial review** (fidelity/correctness + determinism/save) → **no must-fix, no should-fix**; both nits applied (made the `IsNaval` invariant explicit at the `LootShip` gate; added a full-capacity-ceiling test).
**Changed:** `GameLogic/GameSession/Game.cs` (`LootShip` + call at the top of the naval loser branch), `Specification/UnitType.cs`+`Ruleset.cs` (`CaptureGoods`); tests `NavalCombatTests.cs`/`CombatModelTests.cs`; docs `combat.md`/`game-logic.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** **Auto-take what fits** (no loot-chooser UI — faithful for the AI, simplest for the human; the goods just appear in the raider's hold). **Save-stable** (no new field). Loot runs **before** sink/damage so it's preserved on both outcomes, and is gated on the winner's `captureGoods` (a plain transport that wins on defence takes nothing).
**Scheduled next:** **1c-3d — privateers' piracy + Francis Drake** (the privateer *type* already exists + loots; this adds `model.ability.piracy` — attack a rival colonial *without* declaring war, hidden nationality in the notice — and **Francis Drake** +50% offence/defence scoped to privateers; closes Drake from the #3 fathers tracker `86d3b7qxr`) — tracked in `86d3bpzuj`.
**Follow-ups:** 1c-3d (piracy + Drake), 1c-3e colonial-colony capture, drydock-colony repair, naval-preferring AI target refinement (all `86d3bpzuj`); European-diplomacy fathers (Franklin/de Witt); building-grant fathers; Bolívar (SoL).
**Needs you:** Nothing blocking. Privateers and warships (frigate/man-o-war) now seize cargo from ships they beat — a beaten merchantman's goods end up in the raider's hold (up to its capacity) instead of all going to the bottom.

## 2026-06-15 — Ship damage + repair (save v21) [program #1, slice 1c-3b]

**Requested:** "Begin working and DO NOT stop" → the next naval sub-slice and the one that bumps the save format: **ship damage + repair** (`86d3bpzp0`).
**Did:** A beaten ship is no longer always sunk. Now (FreeCol `resolveAttack`) a defeated ship **loses its cargo + everyone aboard** and is **damaged** — the hull limps to **Europe** and sits **under forced repair** for `MaxHitPoints − 1` turns (every classic ship is 6 HP → **5 turns**, healing one per turn via `AdvanceRepairs` in `EndTurn`) — **unless** the loss is **decisive (a great loss)** or there's **no repair location**, when it still **sinks**. While repairing, a ship is pinned to 0 movement and **cannot sail home, take on cargo, or be boarded** (`SailToNewWorld` throws; `CheckBuyEuropeGoods`/`CheckBoard` reject — FreeCol `isReadyToTrade`); the **Europe panel** shows its "under repair (N turns)" countdown and hides its sail/buy controls. `UnitType.MaxHitPoints` now parses the spec `hit-points`. The whole damage/repair path is **deterministic** (no RNG) and a foreign power's ship limps to **its own** Europe — so the human's stream 0 stays byte-stable (ADR-009). **Repair location = Europe only** for now (FreeCol's fallback; no drydock building yet — drydock-colony repair deferred).
**Status:** **491 tests green** (460 L1+L2 + 4 soak + 27 scene; +13 net: `NavalCombatTests` — great-loss sinks / normal loss *damages* (attacker & defender) / damage drops cargo+passengers / repairs 5→0 then sails / can't sail / can't board-or-load / repair-state save round-trip / healthy-fleet byte-identical; `CombatModelTests` ship `hit-points` pinned; `EuropePanelTests.ShipUnderRepair_ShowsNoSailButton`). Build 0/0. Save format **v20 → v21** (additive `SavedUnit.RepairTurns`, omitted when 0 → undamaged fleet byte-identical, pre-v21 loads healthy). Process: FreeCol research (pinned `resolveAttack` sink-vs-damage, `csDamageShip`, `getRepairLocation`, `getTurnsForRepair`, all-ships-6-HP) → implement → **2-lens adversarial review** (FreeCol-fidelity + determinism/save) → **no must-fix, no should-fix**; both nits applied (reworded the `hit-points` inheritance comment; documented the mid-round repair-tick timing). Proactively added the board/buy guards (which the review then confirmed).
**Changed:** `GameLogic/GameSession/Game.cs` (`ResolveLoserOutcome(…, greatLoss)` damage-vs-sink branch, `DamageShip`/`RepairTurnsFor`/`CanRepairAtEurope`, `AdvanceRepairs` + `EndTurn` wiring, movement-reset pin, `SailToNewWorld`/`CheckBuyEuropeGoods`/`CheckBoard` guards), `GameLogic/Units/Unit.cs` (`RepairTurnsRemaining`/`IsUnderRepair`/`ClearCargo`), `Specification/UnitType.cs`+`Ruleset.cs` (`MaxHitPoints`), `Persistence/SaveGame.cs` (v21), `presentation/EuropePanel.cs` (repair row); tests `NavalCombatTests.cs`/`CombatModelTests.cs`/`EuropePanelTests.cs` + 4 version-pin bumps; docs `combat.md`/`save-load.md`/`europe.md`/`game-logic.md`/`presentation.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** **Damage-by-default, sink-on-great-loss/no-repair** (faithful). **Europe is the only repair location** (no drydock building yet — FreeCol's documented fallback; drydock-colony repair = a later sub-slice). Repair **ticks once per round** in `EndTurn` (like `AdvanceSailing`) — a one-tick timing edge vs FreeCol's per-owner-turn heal, documented. Persist via an additive `RepairTurns` (default 0 omitted) so undamaged saves are byte-identical.
**Scheduled next:** **1c-3c — cargo loot** (`captureGoods`: a winning ship with the ability + hold space takes the loser's cargo *before* it sinks/limps; save-stable) — kanban `86d3bpzuj` (the 1c-3c/d/e follow-ups task). Then 1c-3d privateers + Francis Drake, 1c-3e colonial-colony capture.
**Follow-ups:** drydock-colony repair (`model.ability.repairUnits`); the 1c-3c/d/e naval sub-slices (`86d3bpzuj`); a naval-preferring AI target refinement; European-diplomacy fathers (Franklin/de Witt); building-grant fathers; Bolívar (SoL).
**Needs you:** Nothing blocking. Naval combat is now non-lethal-by-default: a beaten ship limps to Europe and repairs over 5 turns (sitting idle in port meanwhile) instead of always sinking — only a crushing defeat sinks it. Ships still always repair at Europe (no colony drydocks yet).

## 2026-06-15 — Naval combat (sink-only) + foreign-AI naval [program #1, slice 1c-3a/a′]

**Requested:** "Begin working and DO NOT stop" → tackled the last big combat piece, 1c-3 naval (`86d3bek5r`). A scope/plan workflow decomposed it into sub-slices; this ships the first, save-stable chunk.
**Did:** **Ship-vs-ship combat.** An armed warship attacks an adjacent enemy ship through the existing `Attack`/`CheckAttack`/`CombatModel` spine, with naval differences: `CombatModel.ResolveNaval` adds the FreeCol **evade** band (a defender slips away — every ship has `evadeAttack`); **no terrain bonus** on open water; a **−12.5%-per-hold-slot cargo penalty** (offence & defence — laden ships fight worse, via `CargoSlotsUsed`); transports (offence 0) can't initiate. A beaten ship is **sunk** (`SinkShip`) — removed with **everyone aboard** (passengers drown; this also fixes a latent orphan where `_units.Remove` left passengers with a dangling `CarrierId`) and its cargo. An evaded attack spends the turn, hurts no one. **Foreign warships at war** now hunt the human's nearest **ship** and attack it (1c-3a′ — narrowed the `RunForeignPowerTurn` naval skip; made `NearestHumanUnit` **domain-matched** so ships hunt ships, land hunts land), on the power's own RNG stream. HUD says "sank"/"evaded". **No save-format change (v20)** — a sunk ship is simply absent, like a slain unit.
**Status:** **478 tests green** (448 L1+L2 + 4 soak + 26 scene; +17 naval: `NavalCombatTests` (sink-on-win / sink-the-attacker-on-loss / evade / passengers-drown-no-orphans / cargo-penalty-counts-goods-only / ships-and-land-can't-attack-each-other / unarmed-can't-attack / replay-stable / foreign-warship-attacks+notice / stream-0) + `CombatModelTests` (`ResolveNaval` band theory + the cargo penalty on both offence & defence)). (The foreign-war soak does NOT exercise ship-vs-ship — foreign powers start with offence-0 transports, so the naval AI path is covered by the dedicated L1 foreign-warship test, not the soak.) Build 0/0. Process: scope/plan workflow (sub-sliced 1c-3 → a/a′/b/c/d/e; a is sink-only, save-stable) → implement → **2-lens adversarial review** → **no must-fix**; applied all 6 should-fix (cargo penalty now goods-only not passengers; a cross-domain attack gate so ships/land can't melee each other on the human UI path; the human attack UI now reports Evade; staged the `.uid` sidecars; corrected the false "soak exercises warships" claim; added a direct `ResolveNaval` band theory). Pushing to `main`; CI to confirm.
**Changed:** `GameLogic/Combat/Combat.cs` (`Evade`, `ResolveNaval`, cargo penalty + `GoodsCarried`); `GameLogic/GameSession/Game.cs` (naval branch in `Attack`, `SinkShip`, the `ResolveLoserOutcome` naval guard, `NearestHumanUnit` domain filter, `RunForeignPowerTurn` skip); `presentation/GameController.cs` (naval notice phrasing); tests `NavalCombatTests.cs` (new); docs `combat.md`/`players.md`/`turns.md`/`game-logic.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** **Sink-only** first cut (FreeCol damages-then-repairs by default; ship damage/repair + the save-format bump is 1c-3b). Naval resolution triggers on a **naval defender** (ship-vs-ship). AI targeting **domain-matched** (resolves the cross-domain oddity cleanly — better than the plan's assumption that `CheckAttack` would block ship-vs-land). Verified the FreeCol evade bands against `SimpleCombatModel.java:626-643`.
**Scheduled next:** **1c-3b — ship damage + repair** (save v21: a damaged ship limps to the nearest drydock/Europe, repairs over ~5 turns; sink only on great-win/no-repair) — the one naval sub-slice that bumps the save format. Then 1c-3c loot, 1c-3d privateers+Drake, 1c-3e colonial-colony capture.
**Follow-ups:** the naval sub-slices above; a naval-preferring AI target refinement; the European-diplomacy fathers (Franklin/de Witt); building-grant fathers; Bolívar (SoL).
**Needs you:** Nothing blocking. Naval combat is live (attack an adjacent enemy ship; war rivals' warships hunt yours). Ships always sink for now — damage/repair (so a beaten ship can limp home) is the very next slice.

## 2026-06-15 — Foreign-power retaliation combat [program #1, slice 1c-2]

**Requested:** "Begin working and DO NOT stop" → continued the roadmap: 1c-2, the payoff of 1c-1's rival rendering (attacking a rival was a no-op).
**Did:** Foreign powers now **fight back**. `RunForeignPowerTurn` gained a war branch: when a power is at `Stance.War` with the human (war only starts when the human attacks it), its **armed** units (offence > 0 — e.g. its starting soldier) hunt the human's nearest unit — attack when adjacent (`AttackHumanUnit`), else step toward it. `AttackHumanUnit` is the foreign sibling of 1b's `RaidHumanUnit`: it targets **human units only** (`NearestHumanUnit`, the shared contract), resolves via the internal `Attack(unit, target, RandomFor(power))` overload (the power's **own** RNG stream — never stream 0), and records a `CombatNotice` (attacker = the European nation id) surfaced in the HUD via the existing 1b path. The war branch is **dormant at peace**, so the existing foreign-power determinism/goldens/soak stay byte-stable. No save-format/RNG-stream-0 surface.
**Status:** **450 tests green** (420 L1+L2 + 4 soak + 26 scene; +6 net: `ForeignCombatTests` (at-war retaliates + notice; not-at-war doesn't attack; at-war-with-no-human-on-map attacks nothing — the human-only contract; replay-stable same-seed; doesn't-touch-stream-0) + `SoakTests.ForeignWar_StaysBounded` (6 seeds × 120 turns, all powers at war: bounded/deterministic/round-trip/no-softlock)). Build 0/0. Process: targeted reads (the 1b pattern transcribed to foreign powers) → implement → **2-lens adversarial review** → **no must-fix** (war branch + human-only filter + stream-0 isolation verified clean); applied its 1 should-fix (the no-human-target test) + 1 nit (test rename: the default stance is Uncontacted, not Peace). Scene suite unaffected (the notice→status-bar path is shared with the 1b native L3). Pushing to `main`; CI to confirm.
**Changed:** `GameLogic/GameSession/Game.cs` (`RunForeignPowerTurn` war branch + `AttackHumanUnit`; `NearestHumanUnit` param renamed brave→hunter), `GameLogic/Combat/CombatNotice.cs` (doc: attacker may be European); tests `ForeignCombatTests.cs` (new), `SoakTests.cs` (+foreign-war soak); docs `combat.md`/`diplomacy.md`/`players.md`/`turns.md`/`game-logic.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** Reused the 1b seam wholesale (human-only target via `NearestHumanUnit`, combat via the internal `Attack` overload on the power's stream, the `CombatNotice` HUD path). War only **wages** (retaliation); the AI doesn't yet **declare** war on its own (no tension source but the human's attack) — deferred. Foreign powers attack **units only**, not colonies (colonial-colony assault is 1c-3).
**Scheduled next:** **1c-3 — naval combat + privateers + Francis Drake + colonial-colony assault/capture** (`86d3bek5r`): the largest combat piece (needs a save-format bump for repair state + a new father). Then **#3 — Founding-Father effects** (`86d3b7qxr`: de Witt/Franklin via stance; production/ability ones).
**Follow-ups:** AI-*declared* war (a tension source beyond the human's attack); foreign-vs-foreign / foreign-vs-native AI combat; foreign AI equipping soldiers from colony stock; native/brave unit art (`86d3bmfcx`).
**Needs you:** Nothing blocking. If you arm a unit and attack a rival European power, expect its soldier to come after you next turns (visible via 1c-1 rendering + the HUD notice). Balance (how hard they retaliate) is FreeCol-shaped and a playtest lever later.

## 2026-06-15 — Rival/own-unit rendering + CheckMove foreign-colony guard [program #1, slice 1c-1]

**Requested:** "Move onto 1c" (foreign-power combat + rival rendering). 1c is multi-part, so I scoped it and shipped the highest-value first chunk.
**Did:** **Made the board legible** (the biggest gap to a playable start/middle) + closed a live bug. Replaced the single `_unitMarker` (drew only the human's *first* on-map unit) with a reconciled **`MapView/UnitLayer`** (`GameController.SyncUnitMarkers`): draws every on-map unit the human can see — own units always; foreign powers + native braves only while their tile is in **live sight** (`Game.IsVisible`, not the explored fog, since units move). Non-human units wear an **owner ring** (`OwnerColorOf`: foreign = `EuropeanNation.Color` parsed to `#rrggbb`; native = a constant; fallback red), the human's units none — so "the other" is highlighted and the existing human-unit goldens stay byte-stable. Added **`CheckMove` guard**: you can only enter a colony you own (`unit.IsNative || colony.OwnerId != unit.OwnerId`) — closes the on-map-attack-UI path of strolling onto an ungarrisoned foreign colony (and keeps braves off all colonies, since a brave's `OwnerId 0` collides with the human's id). Logic-only guard; rendering is presentation (ADR-006). **No save-format/RNG surface.**

Build 0/0. Process: scope/plan workflow (sliced 1c into 1c-1/1c-2/1c-3) → implement → **2-lens adversarial review** → fixed its 1 must-fix (a flaky golden staged via a computed click → re-staged via F5 refresh) + 2 should-fix (foreign-colour path now tested; guard made owner-semantically correct + comment fixed). **Existing 4 goldens byte-stable** (human units unchanged; only the new golden added). Pushing to `main`; CI to confirm.
**Changed:** `presentation/GameController.cs` (UnitLayer + SyncUnitMarkers + OwnerColorOf + NormalizeHex), `presentation/UnitMarker.cs` (OwnerColor + ground ring), `scenes/main.tscn` (UnitLayer node; dropped the single UnitMarker), `GameLogic/GameSession/Game.cs` (CheckMove guard); tests `InputTests.cs`/`MainSceneTests.cs`/`JourneyE2ETests.cs`/`VisualGoldenTests.cs`/`OwnerTests.cs` + new golden PNG; docs `presentation.md`/`combat.md`/`units-movement.md`/`players.md`/`game-logic.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** Owner ring on **non-human units only** (human renders unchanged → existing goldens byte-stable; "highlight the other"). Draw foreign units AND braves when in sight (`IsVisible`) — also makes 1b raids visible. Clean migration (no shim) — the 4 single-marker L3 tests now query `UnitLayer`. Golden refreshed via **F5** (QuickSave→RefreshView) to avoid both End-Turn (moves the brave) and a screen-space click (camera-lag flaky).
**Scheduled next:** **1c-2 — foreign-power retaliation combat** (`86d3bek5r`): an armed foreign soldier retaliates against an adjacent human unit at `Stance.War` via `RunForeignPowerTurn` (mirroring `RunNativeTurn`, on the power's own stream) — needs the foreign AI to have/equip a soldier. Then **1c-3 naval + Drake + colonial-colony assault**, and **#3 Founding-Father effects** (`86d3b7qxr`).
**Follow-ups:** native/brave **unit art** (braves render as a red-disc fallback — `brave.png` absent; cosmetic, an asset task); per-tribe native colours; a human nation colour once nation-selection lands.

## 2026-06-15 — Native AI: movement + raids [program #1, slice 1b]

**Requested:** Continue program #1 → **1b: native AI** — make the inert native `Player` rows act (move + raid), faithful to FreeCol, each on its own RNG stream so the human's stream 0 stays byte-stable; add "you were attacked" feedback so combat is two-way and visible.
**Did:** Natives now take a turn. `RunPlayerTurn` dispatches `PlayerType.Native` → **`RunNativeTurn`**: each brave, in stable by-id order, takes one action — if its **home settlement is Displeased+** (alarm > 600, FreeCol's `secureIndianSettlement` seek-and-destroy gate) it hunts the **nearest human unit** (attack if adjacent via the internal `Attack(brave, target, RandomFor(nativePlayer))` overload, else step toward it), otherwise it **wanders** one tile. Removed the `CheckAttack` "natives don't attack" gate (kept the settlement-assault gate closed); braves target **human units only** (`NearestHumanUnit` filter — the AI's contract, since the engine's `AreEnemies` would also admit rivals). Added a transient, unsaved **`CombatNotice`** channel (`Game.CombatNotices`, cleared each `EndTurn`) that `GameController` formats into the status bar after End Turn ("⚔ The Apache raided your Free Colonist!" / "…fought off…raid"). Hardened `CaptureUnit` against the brave-`OwnerId`-0 capture trap (dormant — braves can't capture in the classic spec). Factored `StepToward(player, unit, goal)` out of the explorer pathing. **No save-format change.**
**Status:** **437 tests green** (412 L1+L2 + 3 soak + 22 scene; +11 net: rewrote "natives can't attack" → can-attack; +`NativeAiTests` (7: determinism, stream-0 isolation, raid+notice, calm=no-notice, legal/bounded movement, no-human-target=no-attack, save round-trip), +2 `CombatTests` (capture-owner regression, raid leaves own alarm unchanged), +1 soak (`NativeRaids_StayBounded…`: 8×150 turns at max alarm, no softlock/runaway/negative + deterministic + round-trip), +1 L3 (raid notice in status bar)). Build 0/0. Process: research workflow → implement → **3-lens adversarial review workflow** (determinism / correctness-FreeCol / tests-architecture) → **no must-fix** (no `_random` leak, no correctness bug — every native path verified on the nation's own stream), applied 2 should-fix + nits. **L4 goldens unchanged** (braves are fog-excluded, never rendered). Pushing to `main`; CI to confirm.
**Changed:** `GameLogic/GameSession/Game.cs` (RunNativeTurn + helpers, gate removal, StepToward, CaptureUnit hardening, CombatNotices); `GameLogic/Combat/CombatNotice.cs` (new); `presentation/GameController.cs` (notice consumption + `FormatCombatNotice`); tests `NativeAiTests.cs` (new), `CombatTests.cs`, `MultiPlayerTests.cs`, `SoakTests.cs`, `InputTests.cs`; docs `natives.md`/`combat.md`/`players.md`/`turns.md`/`game-logic.md`/`presentation.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** Raid threshold = **Displeased** (FreeCol attack gate, not the trade/teach Angry gate — the research conflict resolved to the pinned attack gate). **Per-settlement alarm stands in for nation-level native tension**; **one action/brave/turn**; **target = nearest human unit (omniscient)**. Deferred (faithful but out of slice): colony pillage/capture (`pillageUnprotectedColony`), IndianDemand tribute, gift-bringing, full-allowance movement, fog-limited targeting, nation-level native tension store.
**Scheduled next:** **1c — foreign-power combat + rival rendering** (`86d3bek5r`): rivals drawn when discovered; foreign powers attack at war; naval/foreign-unit combat; includes the `CheckMove`-onto-foreign-colony guard flagged in combat.md. Then **#3 Founding-Father effects** (`86d3b7qxr`).
**Follow-ups:** native colony pillage/capture; IndianDemand/gifts; nation-level native tension; multi-line raid notice (today's status bar shows them joined); fog-limited/scored brave targeting.
**Needs you:** Nothing blocking. Two-way combat is now visible (raid your wandering units / garrisons). Raid frequency/aggression are FreeCol-pinned balance knobs you may want to tune once the UI is richer — a playtest lever, not a blocker.

## 2026-06-15 — FP-7: save-format v20 consolidation (drop legacy flat fields)

**Requested:** "Continue through as you would" (no playtest-gating) → next clean roadmap item: FP-7 (`86d3bex56`).
**Did:** Consolidated the v20 save format — `Players[]` has been the source of truth for player state since FP-1, but `From()` also dual-wrote the legacy flat top-level fields. FP-7 stops writing them:
- The 4 non-nullable flat value fields (`Gold`/`Tax`/`Liberty`/`Immigration`) became `int?`; `From()` no longer writes any flat field (gold/tax/market/liberty/Congress/fathers/immigration/dock/explored); the fold path coalesces with `?? 0` so ≤v19 saves fold identically.
- **`CurrentVersion` stays 20** (deliberate, plan-argued): the v20 load path was already `Players[]`-only, so dropping the redundant mirror changes no branch — new v20 saves are just smaller. Flat *properties* kept (read-only) so ≤v19 saves still fold and pre-FP-7 v20 saves (carry both) still load.
- Process: research workflow (legacy-field map + version-test blast radius → migration plan) → implement → adversarial review (running).
**Status:** **425 tests green** (403 L1+L2 + 2 soak + 20 scene; +7 SaveGameTests: `NewSave_OmitsLegacyFlatPlayerFields`, `LegacyV20Save_...LoadsFromPlayersIgnoringFlatFields`, `OldSaveVersion_WithFlatFields_FoldsToOneHuman` (v9/12/19), `HumanState_RoundTripsThroughPlayersOnly`, `PreV9Save_WithNoTreasuryTokens_FoldsToZeroDefaults`; rewrote `V19Save_LoadsAsSingleHumanPlayer` to construct the v19 shape since `From` no longer emits flat fields). Soak round-trip stays byte-identical (flat fields omitted symmetrically). Persistence-only — no RNG/gameplay change. Adversarial review: **no blockers**; added its suggested pre-v9 absent-token test. Committed; CI running.
**Changed:** `SaveGame.cs` (nullable flat fields + drop writes + fold `?? 0` + version doc); `SaveGameTests.cs` (rewrite 1 + 4 new); docs `save-load.md`/`game-logic.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** Keep Version=20 (no bump — no new load path; a bump would mislead). Keep flat properties read-only (don't delete — needed to deserialize old saves). Losslessness rests on: the fold `?? 0` reproducing the old `default(int)`; the `Players[]` arm ignoring flat fields; the 8 byte-identical round-trip tests as the symmetry net.
**Scheduled next:** the FP wave (FP-1→FP-7) is now **complete**. Next per roadmap: the deferred **Founding-Father effects** (`86d3b7qxr`) and the rest of **FP-6b** (AI acting on stance / native raids — gameplay-altering, pairs with combat UI). Then Phase 6 (independence & REF).
**Needs you:** Nothing for FP-7. The remaining wave-adjacent work (AI combat, founding-father effects) is logic I can continue; the *visible* AI behaviour + UI is where your playtest will matter once there's a combat/rival UI.

## 2026-06-15 — FP-6b (1/n): tension→stance state machine

**Requested:** "Continue with the current correct order of building things" (back on the roadmap after the colony-screen question) → next is finishing FP-6 (`86d3bex51`).
**Did:** Implemented the **tension→stance state machine**, the in-order logic foundation of FP-6b (no playtest needed; the *visible* AI-combat parts come next):
- Added `Stance.CeaseFire` (=3, appended so FP-6a save ordinals stay stable).
- `Game.StanceFromTension(current, tension)` — a faithful port of FreeCol `Stance.getStanceFromTension` (DELTA=10): War→CeaseFire at ≤590, CeaseFire→Peace at ≤90, Peace/CeaseFire→War at >1010, Uncontacted unchanged.
- `UpdateColonialStances` runs each turn in `EndTurn` **after** tension decay, re-deriving each met colonial pair's stance from its cooled tension (so a war drifts war→cease-fire→peace as it cools). Deterministic, no RNG, symmetric, id-ordered.
- Still **recorded-only** — `AreEnemies`/combat/fog/move legality unchanged; the AI doesn't yet *act* on stance.
**Status:** **418 tests green** (396 L1+L2 + 2 soak + 20 scene; +14 incl. an 11-case `StanceFromTension` theory + war→cease-fire / cease-fire→peace integration). Goldens unchanged; byte-stable (no RNG; soak + stream-0 guard hold). Adversarial review: **no blockers / no should-fix** (FreeCol fall-through fidelity + invariants verified). Committed; CI running.
**Changed:** `Stance.cs` (CeaseFire), `Game.cs` (`StanceFromTension`/`UpdateColonialStances` + EndTurn wiring + thresholds); `DiplomacyTests.cs` (+14, 2 FP-6a tests updated for the now-evolving stance); docs `diplomacy.md`/`turns.md`/`players.md`/`save-load.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** Stance update runs after decay (one-turn de-escalation; matches "cool then read"). `CeaseFire` appended (ordinal stability). `Peace→War from tension alone` is implemented faithfully but unreachable in play today (only an attack raises colonial tension, and it sets War directly) — documented + unit-tested. `Alliance` deferred (only a diplomacy action sets it).
**Scheduled next:** the rest of **FP-6b** — the AI *acting* on stance (foreign powers/natives deciding to declare & wage war; native raids; foreign-power-initiated combat, `86d3bek5r`). **This is the gameplay-altering / visible part — best done with your playtest.**
**Needs you:** A steer on the AI-combat scope when ready (raid frequency/targeting/balance are playtest-tuned). Nothing blocking; this slice is invisible in-game.

## 2026-06-14 — FP-5: foreign-power AI economy (trade + immigration + recruit)

**Requested:** Continue the foreign-powers wave → FP-5 (`86d3bex4w`): give the foreign powers an economy, per-player and on their own RNG streams, so the human's stream 0 stays byte-stable.
**Did:**
- **Foreign economy runs:** `RunPlayerTurn` is unified — every colonial player now runs `RunColonyTurn` for its colonies + `AccumulateLibertyAndElectFathers`/`AccumulateImmigrationAndEmigrate` (FP-4 only ran the human's). A foreign power then runs the new **`RunForeignPowerEconomy`**: pursue a Founding Father (pick from its offers), **sell each colony's tradeable surplus — never food — to its OWN market** (`SellColonyGoods(power,…)`), and **recruit** while affordable up to a Europe cap (`AiMaxEuropeRecruits` = 2; no AI shipping yet), then the FP-4 unit AI.
- **Determinism (the crux):** the economy RNG helpers (`GenerateOffers`, `DrawRecruitType`, the emigrate draw, `RefreshDockForRecruitability` via dock draws, + the new father-pick/recruit) now draw from **`RandomFor(player)`** — the human keeps stream 0, foreign powers draw from `Player.Rng`. Founding-father production modifiers fold **per player** (`ApplyGoodsModifiers(player,…)`/`HasAbilityFor(player,…)`; public no-arg overloads delegate to the human, byte-identical). Foreign powers get their **own recruit dock** at `New` (drawn from their own streams) + topped up on load.
- **Owner-id fix:** `CreateEuropeRecruit`/`BuyUnit` (and via the review, `LeaveColony`) now stamp `OwnerId = player.PlayerId` — previously a foreign power's recruits would have wrongly belonged to the human (id 0). Auto-emigration is correctly owned too.
- **Tests:** new `ForeignPowerEconomyTests` (full economy on own stream/market; per-player market **independence**; per-player market **save round-trip**; recruit + owner-id; whole-economy byte-stability) + rewrote the FP-4 "no economy" test; extended the **soak** (no negative treasury, economy active + bounded, 200-turn active-economy round-trip byte-identical, 2 ms budget).
- **Process:** manual deep-read research → implement → 1 thorough adversarial-review agent (**no blockers**; all 5 hard invariants held). Applied its 4 low-sev items: 2 latent seam fixes (`LeaveColony` owner, `Visit` per-player RNG — both byte-identical for the human), ordinal goods sort, DRY `OwnPersonsInEurope`.
**Status:** **388 tests green** (366 L1+L2 incl. 10 E2E +5 new, 2 soak, 16 L3, 4 L4); builds 0/0; goldens unchanged; save **v20**. **Human stream 0 / market / goldens byte-stable**; soak round-trips 25 seeds × 200 turns of active rival economies byte-identically within the 2 ms budget. Pushed to `main` (`2472074`); **CI ✓** (run `27495670975`).
**Changed:** `Game.cs` (economy + per-player RNG/modifier folding + owner stamps + foreign dock), tests `ForeignPowerEconomyTests` (new), `MultiPlayerTests`, `SoakTests`; docs `players.md`/`market.md`/`turns.md`/`immigration.md`/`save-load.md`/`founding-fathers.md`/`QA-REPORT.md`.
**Decisions:** Minimal economy — the AI sells the colony **centre tile's** unattended cash-crop output (it doesn't staff cash-crop tiles or refine); food excluded explicitly (it **is** a tradeable market good in the classic spec, so an `IsTradeable`-only filter would starve colonies). Recruit (not goods-buy) is the Europe action — goods-buying needs a docked AI ship (FP-6). Save stays **v20** (additive; frozen at FP-7).
**Scheduled next:** **FP-6 — AI combat + diplomacy basics** (`86d3bex51`): stance/tension primitives (contact→peace, attack→war, tension→stance) replacing the stubbed `AreEnemies` hook; new `docs/systems/diplomacy.md`; foreign/naval combat lands here.
**Follow-ups (review-flagged, latent):** a human-only baseline regression test pinning stream 0 (architecturally enforced + replay-stable today); the FP-4 carry-overs still open (frontier cache for `StepTowardNearestUnexplored`; min-distance-between-colonies rule; synthesize native `Player` rows on pre-FP-3b load; persist `_currentPlayerIndex`; wire native units to their player id); AI goods-buying + shipping recruits home (FP-6).
**Needs you:** Nothing — no human-facing gameplay/UI change (rival economies are off-screen under your fog). Say the word for FP-6.

## 2026-06-14 — FP-4: minimal foreign-power AI (explore / move / found)

**Requested:** Continue the foreign-powers wave → FP-4 (`86d3bex4u`), the first active AI.
**Did:**
- The 3 foreign powers go **active**: at `Game.New` they **land on the map** far from the human (`LandForeignPower` — deterministic, no RNG) instead of docking in Europe; each non-human player gets its **own PCG stream** (`Player.Rng`, saved/restored; save v20 additive `RngState`/`RngIncrement`).
- **AI** (`RunForeignPowerTurn`): per unit by-id — a colonist founds where it stands while under `MaxAiColonies`, else steps toward the nearest tile it hasn't explored (`StepTowardNearestUnexplored`); ships idle. A flat priority switch, **not** a planner. All choices draw from the player's **own** stream (`RandomFor`) — never the human's stream 0.
- Fog generalised to the unit's owner (`RevealForOwner`): foreign powers explore under their own fog; the human can't see/manage a foreign colony until discovered (presentation **fog-gates** colony markers + only the human's own colonies are clickable/HUD/camera — closed 2 owner-leaks the review flagged).
- Process: 3-reader research workflow (test blast-radius + AI helpers/fog + per-player-RNG design) → implement → adversarial review (clean on determinism/save/AI-correctness; fixed its 2 presentation findings).
**Status:** **384 tests green** (363 logic incl. new AI + replay-stability tests, 20 scene/golden, 2 soak); CI ✓ (run `27494340531`); save **v20**; git clean on `main`. **Byte-stable + replay-stable**: human stream 0 / RNG-resume / L4 goldens unchanged; **soak round-trips 25 seeds × 200 turns of active rivals byte-identically within the 2 ms turn budget**; two same-seed games byte-identical after 20 AI turns.
**Changed:** `Game.cs` (landing + AI + per-player RNG + owner-reveal), `Player.cs` (`Rng`), `SaveGame.cs` (per-player RNG), `GameController.cs` (fog-gate + owner-gated click/HUD/focus); tests `MultiPlayerTests`/`OwnerTests`/`JourneyTests`; docs `players.md`/`turns.md`/`save-load.md`. Commit `0ad7cef`.
**Decisions:** Foreign powers **land on the map** (not Europe) — required for the explore/found AI; far landing + the colony-marker fog-gate keep the goldens stable (no regeneration). AI is **deterministic + per-player-streamed** (replay-stable, stream 0 untouched). Natives stay **inert** (their AI is a later slice). Save stays **v20** (additive through the wave, frozen at FP-7).
**Scheduled next:** **FP-5 — AI economy: trade + immigration + recruit** (`86d3bex4w`) — give the foreign powers an economy on their own streams/markets.
**Follow-ups (review-flagged, latent):** owner-gate the rest of the presentation when rivals become discoverable; `StepTowardNearestUnexplored` is O(map)/unit/turn (fine at the 2 ms budget, cache a frontier when the AI grows); min-distance-between-colonies rule (the `CheckFoundColony` TODO); synthesize native `Player` rows on a pre-FP-3b load; wire native units to their player id.

**Requested:** Start the foreign-powers wave with FP-1 (`86d3bex4a`) — pure refactor: move player-scoped state off `Game` onto a new `Player`, save v20, all tests stay green.

- Mutating seams (`Sell*`/`Buy*`/`Recruit`/`Visit`/`SellToNatives`/accumulate/`Reveal`) got internal `Player`-taking overloads; the public method delegates to `HumanPlayer`. Collapsed the 23-param `Game.Restore` to take a `RestoredPlayer` list (one element).

A v20 save still writes the flat fields too (every pre-v20 load path stays exercised; dropped at FP-7). New test `V19Save_LoadsAsSingleHumanPlayer`; determinism/goldens/soak untouched (human = RNG stream 0).

Founding-father modifier/ability resolution + live `CurrentlyVisible` stay human-scoped in FP-1 (need the owner-id seam — FP-2); only the **stored** fog (`Explored`) is per-player now.

## 2026-06-14 — Combat 5c: native settlement assault (Phase 5 slice 5c)

**Requested:** Move onto the next item of work (Combat 5c).
**Did:**
- Native **settlement assault**: `CheckAttackSettlement`/`AttackSettlement` — a settlement defends with an implicit garrison (brave defence × its settlement defence bonus); a win sacks it (destroy + plunder gold), a loss disarms/demotes the attacker. Can't walk onto a native settlement.
- `<plunder>` ranges parsed (`SettlementPlunder`); `ComputePlunder` ≈ FreeCol `RandomRange`; **Hernán Cortés** (`plunderNatives`) → the richer "extra" range. Save **v19** (marker only — a destroyed settlement is just absent; plunder folds into gold).
- Process: research workflow (4 readers, numbers verified vs `freecol/`) → implement → adversarial review workflow → fixed both confirmed findings.
- **Review fix + a deeper unification:** reworked combat tension to FreeCol's `defenderTension` across **both** 5b (open-field) and 5c (settlement): a win raises the nation's alarm nation-wide (open kill +500; non-capital sack +900; burning a capital → the nation **surrenders**, settlements set to 350); a **repelled** attack *lowers* it (−100, or −300 if your unit is slain). This corrects 5b's flat +200/+400 (wrong-signed on a loss) and a false "destroy adds none" doc claim.
**Status:** **366 tests green** (346 logic incl. 2 soak + 20 scene); CI running; pushed to `main` (`d8d6631`).
**Changed:** `Game.cs`, `NativeNationType.cs` (+`SettlementPlunder`), `Ruleset.cs`, `NativeSettlement.cs`, `SaveGame.cs`; tests `CombatTests.cs` (+ version pins); 7 docs synced (combat, natives, save-load, ruleset-data, founding-fathers, game-logic, QA-REPORT).
**Decisions:** Scoped 5c to **settlement assault only** — naval + foreign-European + native-initiated (AI) combat have no targets natives-only, so they move to the foreign-powers slice; **implicit-garrison** defender (vs braves-adjacent), justified by avoiding an in-settlement unit-list + save-schema change.
**Scheduled next:** **Foreign European powers + multi-player refactor + basic AI** (`86d3b7qwm`) — the largest Phase 5 chunk; decompose when reached. (It also unlocks naval/foreign-unit combat + Drake, tracked at `86d3bek5r`.)
**Follow-ups:** Naval + foreign-unit combat (+ Drake); native-initiated raids (AI); apply role movement bonuses (`86d3bbvv6`); deferred Founding-Father effects (`86d3b7qxr`).
**Needs you:** Still no combat UI (logic + tests only). Phase 5's remaining big piece (foreign powers + AI) is large — say if you'd rather I decompose it into a plan first, or push on.
